TL;DR: 本文提出TDVR，一个无需训练的推理框架，用于零样本3D视觉定位。通过构建语义3D场景图、利用LLM化解模糊查询、推理最优视角以及区分相似物体，在ScanRefer数据集上分别以15.25%和14.46%的提升超越了现有最先进方法（Acc@0.25和Acc@0.5）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有零样本3D视觉定位方法受限于模糊的查询文本和不足的视角，导致定位效果不佳。作者旨在解决这两个关键瓶颈，提升零样本场景下的3D目标定位能力。

Method: TDVR包含四个关键步骤：(1) 从3D点云检测实例构建语义3D场景图；(2) 将原始查询、外观和空间关系描述输入LLM进行融合消歧，并通过思维链推理生成结构化查询表示；(3) 基于场景图和结构化查询进行视角推理，获得最优视角；(4) 通过视角分数、混淆分数、类别分数和外观分数的集成匹配来确定目标物体。

Result: 在公共ScanRefer数据集上，TDVR在Acc@0.25和Acc@0.5指标上分别超过现有最先进方法15.25%和14.46%，验证了该方法在视角推理、相似物体判别和模糊查询理解方面的优势。

Conclusion: TDVR通过训练自由的推理框架有效解决了零样本3D视觉定位中模糊查询和视角不足的问题，在实验中显著优于已有方法，展示了该框架在3D视觉定位任务中的强大潜力。

Abstract: Zero-shot 3D visual grounding aims to localize specific objects based on textual descriptions and 3D visual input. However, the effectiveness of existing methods is significantly hindered by the ambiguous query text and deficient viewpoints. To address these issues, we propose TDVR, a training-free reasoning framework that disambiguates the input text and infers accurate viewpoints for zero-shot 3D visual grounding. First, we construct semantic 3D scene graph from the detected instances in the 3D point cloud. Subsequently, we put the original query, appearance and spatial relationship descriptions into the LLM for fusion, thereby disambiguating the initial input. We leverage chain-of-thought reasoning to generate the structured representation of disambiguated query. Then taking the scene graph and structured query as input, we get the optimal view via viewpoint reasoning to solve the problem of missing viewpoints during grounding. Based on the obtained optimal viewpoint, we further discriminate the distracting objects, enabling the model with the ability to distinguish similar instances. After that, we match the category text and appearance images with the query by computing the similarity of feature vectors. Finally, the target object was identified by integrating the viewpoint score, confusion score, category score, and appearance score. Compared with previous methods, our TDVR has stronger capabilities in viewpoint reasoning, similar object discrimination, and ambiguous query understanding. Experimental results on the public ScanRefer dataset show that our method outperforms the existing state-of-the-art methods by 15.25% and 14.46% in Acc@0.25 and Acc@0.5 respectively, demonstrating the effectiveness of our TDVR in addressing ambiguous query text and deficient viewpoints.

</details>


### [102] [AgenticVAU: Multi-Agent Explore-Verify Reasoning for Video Anomaly Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03779)
*Yuxiang Duan,Huining Li,Ao Li,Shuai Feng,Lanju Kong,Ning Liu,Jian Zhang,Xingdong Sheng,Yuntao Du*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: AgenticVAU是一个无训练的多智能体框架，将视频异常理解（VAU）建模为"探索-验证"过程，通过四个专业化智能体（视觉规则构建、搜索规划、视频观察、最终决策）协同工作，并利用锚点注册表共享证据记忆，在ECVA、UCF-Crime和MSAD基准上超越了零样本推理和强化学习基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VAU方法要么依赖专门训练导致泛化受限，要么使用单智能体将探索、观察和决策混为一体，缺乏角色分工和结构化证据协调。作者旨在解决这些问题，提出一个无需训练的、具有角色专业化分工的多智能体框架。

Method: AgenticVAU将VAU重构为探索-验证（explore-verify）过程：系统先发现潜在异常，再通过针对性观察进行验证。框架包含四个专业智能体——视觉规则构建、搜索规划、视频观察和最终决策，它们通过锚点注册表（anchor registry）这一共享证据记忆进行通信，交替执行广泛的时序探索、密集的局部验证和跨区间比较，直至收集到足够证据。

Result: 在VAU-Bench的ECVA、UCF-Crime和MSAD三个子集上进行了广泛实验，AgenticVAU在性能上超越了零样本推理和基于强化学习的基线方法，验证了多智能体协作在视频异常理解中的价值。

Conclusion: 多智能体协作框架通过角色专业化和结构化证据协调，能够有效提升视频异常理解的性能，且无需额外的专门训练，展示了训练免费方法在该任务上的潜力。

Abstract: Video anomaly understanding (VAU) focuses on comprehensively interpreting abnormal events in videos, requiring models to identify anomalous occurrences, discover their supporting evidence, and explain the underlying causes beyond simple anomaly detection. Existing VAU methods often rely on specialized training or limited observations, restricting generalization or evidence coverage. Although single-agent alternatives support adaptive video observation, they still integrate exploration, observation, and decision-making within a unified reasoning process, offering limited role specialization and structured evidence coordination. To address these limitations, we present AgenticVAU, a training-free multi-agent framework that casts VAU as an explore--verify process, where the system first discovers potential anomalies and then verifies them through targeted observations. To achieve this, four specialized agents are introduced to handle visual-rule construction, search planning, video observation, and final decision, respectively. These agents communicate through an anchor registry, a shared evidence memory that binds each observation. Guided by this agent framework, AgenticVAU interleaves broad temporal exploration, dense local verification, and cross-interval comparison until sufficient evidence is collected. We conduct extensive experiments on the ECVA, UCF-Crime, and MSAD subsets of VAU-Bench, the results show that AgenticVAU outperforms zero-shot inference and reinforcement learning-based baselines, demonstrating the value of multi-agent collaboration for video anomaly understanding.

</details>


### [103] [OmniPack: Unified Token Compression for Efficient Omni-modal Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03812)
*Wanshun Su,Yang Shi,Feihu Liu,Ziwen Yu,Yan Min,Zhuoran Zhang,Qixun Wang,Haotian Wang,Shixuan Liu,Yuanxing Zhang,Peng Wu,Chengfu Huo,Liang Ding*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: OmniPack是一种无需训练的框架，通过LLM前的结构性压缩（模态重要性、全局覆盖、相似度感知合并）与LLM内的任务相关语义精炼（文本引导、音视频协作）相结合，在低token预算下实现Omni-LLM的最佳性能-效率权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的Omni-LLM在处理长且高冗余的视觉和音频token序列时计算开销巨大，需要激进压缩。但现有方法在低token预算下性能退化严重：LLM前压缩可能丢弃结构重要和全局分布的证据，LLM内压缩则未充分利用查询条件下的音视频协作。

Method: OmniPack采用两阶段协调策略：1) LLM之前，通过模态特定重要性、全局覆盖和相似度感知合并来移除结构冗余；2) 多模态交互充分后，通过文本引导和音视频协作进一步整合多样化、任务相关的表示。整个框架训练免费。

Result: 在五个基准测试、三个Omni-LLM骨干上，OmniPack在不同保留比率下均取得最佳性能-效率权衡。在Qwen2.5-Omni-7B上，将FLOPs降至16.7%时保持98.0%的原始性能；仅用6.8%的FLOPs仍保留92.9%的性能。

Conclusion: OmniPack通过协调LLM前的结构压缩与LLM内的语义精炼，有效解决了现有方法在激进token压缩下的性能退化问题，实现了显著的效率提升同时保持高保真性能。

Abstract: Omni-modal large language models (Omni-LLMs) have achieved remarkable performance on audio-visual understanding tasks, but processing long and highly redundant visual and audio token sequences incurs substantial computational overhead, demanding aggressive token compression for efficient deployment. Existing methods often degrade at low token budgets: pre-LLM compression may discard structurally important and globally distributed evidence, whereas inner-LLM compression often underexploits query-conditioned audio-visual collaboration. To address these limitations, we propose OmniPack, a training-free framework that coordinates structural compression before the LLM with task-relevant semantic refinement within the LLM. Before the LLM, OmniPack removes structural redundancy through modality-specific importance, global coverage, and similarity-aware merging. After sufficient multimodal interaction, it further consolidates diverse, task-relevant representations through textual guidance and audio-visual collaboration. Extensive experiments on five benchmarks with three Omni-LLM backbones demonstrate that OmniPack consistently achieves the best performance-efficiency trade-off across diverse retention ratios, outperforming all existing methods. Notably, on Qwen2.5-Omni-7B, OmniPack preserves 98.0% of the original performance while reducing FLOPs to 16.7%, and still retains 92.9% of the original performance with only 6.8% of the original FLOPs.

</details>


### [104] [UHP Detection: LVLMs have their Unique Hallucination Pattern in the Consistency Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03817)
*Amir Mohammad Ezzati,Kiyan Rezaee,Bardiya Kariminia,Mohamad Amin Yousefi,Asal Mohammadjafari Mamaqani,Behrad Samimi,Mohammad Hossein Rohban*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出UHP Detection，一种全黑盒的大视觉语言模型幻觉检测框架，通过扰动模态（图像vs文本）和逻辑极性（陈述vs否定）两个轴构建四组互补的一致性组，提取组内和组间特征训练轻量级分类器，在AMBER和PhD基准上超越现有黑盒和白盒方法，AUC-ROC最高提升+18.72%，AUC-PR最高提升+20.07%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有黑盒幻觉检测方法仅通过单一一致性指标估计不确定性，但幻觉在不同行为探测下表现出多样化的不确定性表现，单一度量不足以刻画其潜在行为特征。

Method: 提出UHP Detection框架：将幻觉建模为由两个轴定义的结构化不确定模式——扰动模态（图像vs文本）和逻辑极性（陈述vs其否定），二者交叉形成四个互补一致性组，从中提取组内和组间特征训练轻量级分类器。

Result: 在AMBER和PhD基准上、跨三个LVLM的实验显示，UHP Detection一致优于现有黑盒和白盒基线，AUC-ROC最高提升+18.72%，AUC-PR最高提升+20.07%。消融实验表明各一致性组提供互补信息，跨数据集评估显示学到的模式可泛化。

Conclusion: 幻觉行为反映一种模型特定的结构化一致性模式，多维度交叉探测比单一一致性度量更能全面刻画幻觉不确定性；该模式可跨基准泛化，代码已开源。

Abstract: Large vision--language models (LVLMs) demonstrate strong multimodal reasoning capabilities but remain prone to hallucination, where model predictions are not grounded in visual evidence. Existing black-box hallucination detection methods estimate uncertainty through a single consistency metric, implicitly assuming that model uncertainty can be adequately characterized by a single measure. However, hallucinations exhibit diverse manifestations of uncertainty across different behavioral probes, making a single measure insufficient to characterize their underlying behavior. We propose \emph{Unique Hallucination Pattern (UHP) Detection}, a fully black-box framework that models hallucination as a structured uncertainty pattern defined by two axes: perturbation modality (image vs.\ text) and logical polarity (a statement vs.\ its negation). Their intersection produces four complementary consistency groups that capture distinct manifestations of model uncertainty, from which both within-group and between-group features are extracted to train a lightweight classifier. Through comprehensive experiments on AMBER and PhD across three LVLMs, UHP Detection consistently outperforms prior black-box and white-box baselines, with improvements of up to $+18.72\%$ AUC-ROC and $+20.07\%$ AUC-PR over the strongest black-box methods. Extensive ablation studies demonstrate that each consistency group contributes complementary information and that their combination forms a structured hallucination pattern. Furthermore, cross-dataset evaluation shows that this learned pattern generalizes across benchmarks, indicating that hallucination behavior reflects a model-specific consistency pattern. \textbf{Code is publicly available at} https://github.com/amirezzati/uhpdet.

</details>


### [105] [FlowForm: Synergizing Fluid Physics with Topological Consistency for Satellite Flood Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03822)
*Zhang Weihui,Wang Ruizhi,Xu Hongye,Wang Huiqiong,Sun Li,Song Mingli*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出FlowForm框架，用于卫星洪水图像合成，通过SWE启发的潜在正则化与结构感知条件化，以生成空间布局合理、场景结构保真的洪水后卫星图像，并配套构建了大规模高分辨率配对数据集FloodScape。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高质量的配对卫星影像对于构建稳健的洪水评估模型至关重要，但此类数据稀缺。虽然生成模型可用于数据增强，现有方法常产生不合理的洪水区域空间布局并扭曲场景结构。

Method: FlowForm包含两个核心模块：(1) Flood Descriptor Module (FDM)，在扩散模型瓶颈处的辅助潜在场中，对稳态浅水方程残差施加可微惩罚；(2) Terrain Anchor Adapter (TAA)，在U-Net四个编码器尺度注入深度、语义和边缘特征。此外构建了高分辨率灾前灾后配对数据集FloodScape。

Result: 在标准图像生成指标、洪水区域一致性、对地理上未见洪灾事件的零样本泛化以及各组件敏感性分析等维度上，FlowForm均取得更高视觉保真度、更强的配对图像相似性和更一致的洪水区域效果。

Conclusion: FlowForm通过物理启发的潜在正则化和多尺度结构条件注入，有效解决了洪水卫星图像合成中空间布局不合理与结构失真问题，为洪水评估模型提供了更高质量的数据增强方案。

Abstract: Developing robust flood assessment models requires high-quality paired satellite imagery, yet such data remain scarce for flood-specific image generation. Although generative models provide a promising means of data augmentation, existing methods often yield implausible spatial layouts of flooded regions and distort scene structures. We propose FlowForm, a framework for satellite flood synthesis that integrates SWE-inspired latent regularization with structure-aware conditioning. The Flood Descriptor Module (FDM) imposes differentiable penalties on residuals of the steady-state Shallow Water Equation in auxiliary latent fields at the diffusion bottleneck. The Terrain Anchor Adapter (TAA) injects depth, semantic, and edge features at four encoder scales of the U-Net. We further curate FloodScape, a large-scale, high-resolution dataset comprising paired satellite images acquired before and after disasters. In addition to standard image-generation metrics, we evaluate the consistency of flooded regions, zero-shot generalization to a geographically held-out flood event, and sensitivity to individual components. Across all reported comparisons, FlowForm achieves higher visual fidelity, greater similarity between paired images, and stronger consistency of flooded regions.

</details>


### [106] [Geo-Embed: Towards Unified Multimodal Embeddings for Urban Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03826)
*Jiapeng Li,Yong Li,Junjie Zhou,Fan Zhang,Yu Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出GeoMEB基准和Geo-Embed模型，标准化45个城市多模态嵌入任务，覆盖检索、VQA、变化检测、分类和视觉定位等，包含132万训练样本和28.6万评估查询，Geo-Embed在统一嵌入空间中实现跨异构地理空间输入的查询-目标匹配，相对最强基线提升15.3%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态嵌入模型和基准主要围绕通用图像-文本匹配设计，无法清晰验证统一嵌入空间能否支持涉及空间关系、细粒度语义和时间变化的地理空间异构任务，存在评估与建模空白。

Method: 提出GeoMEB大规模多模态嵌入基准（45个城市任务、132万训练样本、28.6万评估查询）；提出Geo-Embed统一嵌入模型，基于共享视觉-语言骨干，通过指令条件化的查询-目标匹配来处理异构地理空间输入（单图、多图、文本、区域、掩码）。

Result: Geo-Embed在GeoMEB上取得最强综合性能，相对最强基线提升15.3%。

Conclusion: 结果表明未来地理空间嵌入模型应围绕显式查询-目标关系（语义、跨视角、区域级和时间对应）来组织训练与评估。

Abstract: Geospatial and urban applications increasingly require models to compare heterogeneous evidence across street-view imagery, remote-sensing observations, text descriptions, region proposals, and temporal change cues. However, existing multimodal embedding models and benchmarks are still largely designed and evaluated around general-purpose image-text matching, leaving unclear whether unified embedding space can support heterogeneous geospatial tasks involving spatial relationships, fine-grained semantics, and temporal changes. To address this gap, we make three key contributions. First, we introduce GeoMEB, a large-scale multimodal embedding benchmark that standardizes 45 urban evaluation tasks across retrieval, visual question answering, change detection, classification, and visual grounding, together with training collections comprising 1.32M examples and 286K evaluation queries. Second, we present Geo-Embed, a unified embedding model that adapts a shared vision-language backbone to instruction-conditioned query-target matching over heterogeneous geospatial inputs, including single images, multiple images, text, regions, and masks. On GeoMEB, Geo-Embed achieves the strongest overall performance among representative multimodal embedders, with a 15.3% relative improvement over the strongest baseline. These results motivate future geospatial embedders that organize training and evaluation around explicit query-target relations, including semantic, cross-view, region-level, and temporal correspondence.

</details>


### [107] [LiteMVS: Efficient Multi-View Stereo with Foundation Distillation and Expert Aggregation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03851)
*Tianbao Zhang,Zeyu Liu,Shuyu Wu,Fanxing Li,Zhaoxin Fan,Wenjun Wu,Danping Zou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: LiteMVS是一个轻量级多视图深度估计模型，将平面扫描几何推理与单目语义和结构先验相结合，通过语义描述符增强代价体、MoE实现自适应几何聚合，以及从视觉基础模型蒸馏几何先验，在静态场景深度估计和3D重建中取得高质量结果，同时为后续4D表示学习奠定几何基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MVS方法依赖几何对应关系，在无纹理或重复区域容易失效；单目深度模型虽有图像级先验但缺乏多视图几何约束。此外，在机器人和具身智能场景中，高质量3D几何不仅是静态重建所需，更是学习时序一致的4D表示的关键基础，因此需要一种兼具结构感知能力和时空扩展潜力的视觉表示。

Method: LiteMVS将平面扫描几何推理与单目先验集成到MVS框架中：1）利用轻量分割模型和大规模视觉基础模型注入高层单目知识；2）用语义描述符丰富代价体；3）采用MoE（Mixture-of-Experts）实现跨深度假设的自适应几何聚合；4）从视觉基础模型蒸馏几何先验，在不增加推理成本的情况下增强单目引导。

Result: 在ScanNetv2和7-Scenes数据集上，LiteMVS实现了高质量的深度预测和3D重建，同时保持了具有竞争力的效率。

Conclusion: LiteMVS不仅提升了静态场景中的深度估计和3D重建质量，还为后续时间建模和4D表示学习提供了更可靠的几何基础，证明了单目先验与多视图几何融合的有效性。

Abstract: Real-time 3D perception is crucial for robotics, augmented reality, and embodied intelligence applications. Existing multi-view stereo (MVS) methods primarily rely on geometric correspondences, which often fail in textureless or repetitive regions, while monocular depth models leverage strong image-level priors but lack robust multi-view geometric constraints. More importantly, in robotics and embodied manipulation scenarios, high-quality 3D geometry is not only essential for static reconstruction, but also serves as a critical foundation for learning temporally consistent 4D representations. To obtain visual representations with stronger structural awareness and greater potential for spatiotemporal extension, we present LiteMVS, a lightweight multi-view depth estimation model that integrates plane-sweep geometric reasoning with strong monocular semantic and structural priors. The central idea of LiteMVS is to efficiently inject high-level monocular knowledge, obtained from lightweight segmentation models and large-scale vision foundation models, into a multi-view stereo framework. In particular, LiteMVS enriches the cost volume with semantic descriptors and employs a Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) formulation to enable adaptive geometric aggregation across depth hypotheses. Moreover, geometric priors distilled from vision foundation models further strengthen monocular guidance without increasing inference cost. Through this design, LiteMVS not only improves depth estimation and 3D reconstruction quality in static scenes, but also provides a more reliable geometric foundation for subsequent temporal modeling and 4D representation learning. Experiments on ScanNetv2 and 7-Scenes demonstrate that LiteMVS achieves high-quality depth prediction and 3D reconstruction while maintaining competitive efficiency.

</details>


### [108] [CPrefix: A Combinatorial Tensor Framework for Structured Discrete Color Mappings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03863)
*Yvan Richard*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出CPrefix，一种基于组合可观测量的离散多通道映射的表示框架，通过计数张量和离散Pascal单纯形实现对映射的表示、重建和结构分析，并在ICC显示和打印机配置文件中验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的离散多通道映射通过采样值表示，虽然评估准确但难以揭示其底层结构。作者希望找到一种既能准确表示离散映射又能分析其内在结构的组合可观测表示方法。

Method: 提出CPrefix框架，采用统一张量框架，基于多项式计数可观测诱导的计数张量，其支撑形成离散Pascal单纯形作为潜在组合表示，将映射的组合组织与测量值分离，从而实现映射的表示、重建和结构分析。

Result: 在ICC显示和打印机配置文件上验证了该框架，通过潜在重建和感知色域传输实现了精确重建，证明颜色映射具有忠实的可观测表示，重建残差可用于分析映射与所提出表示的兼容性。

Conclusion: 虽然该框架在颜色变换上得到验证，但其与观测量的物理解释无关，适用于颜色成像、光谱测量及其他离散系统中的结构化多通道映射。

Abstract: Discrete multi-channel mappings are typically represented through sampled values, providing accurate evaluations but limited insight into their underlying structure. We introduce CPrefix, a combinatorial observable representation for discrete mappings, realized within a unified tensor framework that enables representation, reconstruction, and structural analysis.
  The framework is based on a counting tensor induced by multinomial counting observables. Its support forms a discrete Pascal simplex, not as a constraint on the observable space, but as a latent combinatorial representation from which mappings are reconstructed. This formulation separates the combinatorial organization of a mapping from its measured values, exposing the observable structure underlying the mapping.
  The framework is validated on ICC display and printer profiles through latent reconstruction and perceptual gamut transport. Accurate reconstruction demonstrates that color mappings admit faithful observable representations, while reconstruction residuals provide insight into the compatibility of the underlying mapping with the proposed representation.
  Although demonstrated on color transformations, the framework is independent of the physical interpretation of the observables, making it applicable to structured multi-channel mappings arising from color imaging, spectral measurements and other discrete systems.

</details>


### [109] [BanglaWild: An In-the-Wild Bengali Scene Text Recognition Benchmark for OCR and Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03884)
*Sadab Shiper,Tawsif Tashwar Dipto,Mir Md Inzamam,Eshat Tanzeem*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了BANGLAWILD基准，包含2,535张孟加拉语场景文本图像及其丰富标注，系统评估了15个VLM和3个传统OCR系统，揭示了"同族大模型未必优于小模型"的发现，并通过对十五类错误分类分析发现视觉误识别占主导（约60%），挑战了孟加拉语OCR研究中长期关注的辅音连字错误假设。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的孟加拉语场景文本识别研究存在明显空白：已有资源主要针对手写文档或受限的招牌解析，仅报告聚合编辑距离指标，且从未在相同的真实场景数据上同时评估传统OCR和视觉语言模型，缺乏系统性的基准对比。

Method: 构建BANGLAWILD基准（2,535张图像，含逐字转录、两个分类轴、四个诊断属性和正字法标准形式）；在三种提示策略下评估15个VLM和3个传统OCR系统；对6个开源模型进行LoRA微调；使用LLM-as-a-Judge补充编辑距离评估；构建十五类错误分类体系进行错误分析。

Result: 同一模型家族中较大模型并不优于较小模型；最强系统的错误中约60%源于视觉误识别，而辅音连字相关错误占比不足2%，挑战了孟加拉语OCR研究的长期假设；视觉主导的错误分布跨架构保持一致；提示语言主要影响跨脚本漂移；LoRA能减少弱模型的灾难性失败但无法提升已具竞争力的模型上限。

Conclusion: 孟加拉语场景文本识别的核心瓶颈在于视觉特征识别而非语言学层面的连字处理，未来研究应聚焦于提升模型的视觉感知能力；同时模型规模不是性能的保证，需要更精细的架构与训练策略考量。

Abstract: In-the-wild Bengali scene text recognition is largely unmeasured: existing resources target handwritten documents or constrained sign-board parsing, report only aggregate edit-distance metrics, and evaluate either conventional OCR or VLMs, never both on the same in-the-wild data. To address this gap, we introduce BANGLAWILD, a benchmark of 2,535 Bengali scene text images, each paired with a verbatim gold transcription, two categorical axes, four diagnostic attributes, and an orthographically standard form where the in-image text deviates from canonical spelling. We evaluate fifteen VLMs and three conventional OCR systems under three prompting strategies, fine-tune 6 open-source models with LoRA, and complement edit-distance metrics with an LLM-as-a-Judge evaluation. Our results reveal a persistent gap in which larger models within the same family do not outperform smaller ones. Our fifteen-class error taxonomy shows that visual mis-recognition accounts for ~60% of errors in the strongest systems, while conjunct-related errors contribute under 2%, challenging a long-standing assumption in Bengali OCR research; the same visual dominant profile also holds across architectures, including the one conventional baseline that reads Bengali reliably. Prompt language mainly affects cross-script drift and LoRA reduces catastrophic failures in weak models without lifting the ceiling on already competent ones. Code and data will be publicly released.

</details>


### [110] [MuRA: Multi-Rank Adaptation for Efficient and Effective Test-Time Vision-Language Generalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03885)
*Gengyuan Liu,Nanzhou Wang,Chang Liu,Qinwen Wu,Zhenhao Wang,Jiacong Wang,Bokui Chen,Xiangyang Ji*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出MuRA框架，通过动态选择并融合不同秩的适配模块来解决视觉-语言模型在测试时适应中固定秩配置导致的性能瓶颈问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有测试时适应方法采用静态秩配置（固定Low-Rank Adaptation秩），但视觉输入天然具有不同的信息密度，固定秩会导致在复杂场景下欠拟合、简单场景下过拟合的优化折中问题。

Method: 提出MuRA框架，包含两大核心组件：（1）Multi-Rank Orthogonal Decomposition——多秩正交分解提供知识保留的初始化；（2）Unified Component Fusion with Continuous Router Updating——统一组件融合与连续路由更新以学习语义到秩的映射。同时提供理论证明支撑自适应机制的数学必要性和梯度稳定性。

Result: 在广泛域泛化和跨数据集基准测试中达到最先进的准确率，同时显著降低计算和内存开销。

Conclusion: MuRA的动态秩设计在深层视觉层表现最佳，利用最短的梯度反向传播路径，验证了自适应秩选择机制的有效性和高效性。

Abstract: Vision-language models exhibit remarkable zero-shot capabilities but suffer significant performance degradation under distribution shifts. While test-time adaptation (TTA) via Low-Rank Adaptation offers a parameter-efficient solution, we identify a fundamental bottleneck in current methods: the reliance on static rank configurations. Because visual inputs inherently possess varying information densities, a fixed rank forces an inevitable optimization compromise, leading to underfitting on complex scenes and overfitting on simple ones. To bridge this gap, we propose Multi-Rank Adaptation (MuRA), a novel framework that dynamically selects and fuses adaptation modules of varying capacities based on token-level visual complexity. MuRA synergizes Multi-Rank Orthogonal Decomposition to provide a superior, knowledge-preserving initialization, and Unified Component Fusion with Continuous Router Updating to sustainably learn semantic-to-rank mappings. Furthermore, we provide rigorous theoretical justifications mathematically proving the necessity and gradient stability of this adaptive mechanism. Crucially, MuRA's dynamic design uniquely thrives at the deepest visual layer, capitalizing on the shortest gradient backpropagation path. Extensive experiments demonstrate that MuRA achieves state-of-the-art accuracy across extensive domain generalization and cross-dataset benchmarks while significantly reducing both computational and memory overhead.

</details>


### [111] [CARE-X: Towards Clinically Useful Radiology VLMs with Auxiliary Supervision, Reward-Aligned Learning, and Tool-Augmented Measurement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03890)
*Mercy Prasanna Ranjit,Anirban Porya,Sathvik Joel,Niharika Vadlamudi,Nikhilesh Chowdary Eathamukkala,Prasanth V,Abhyuday Kumara Swamy,Pranay Narhari Umredkar,Pradeep Narayan,Vivek Rajagopal,Tanuja Ganu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CARE-X是一个胸部X光视觉语言模型，通过将判别式辅助监督（焦点损失分类头和组合损失定位头）与基于奖励的对齐生成（DAPO）统一起来，弥合了放射科医生实际需求与生成模型能力之间的差距，在报告生成、VQA和空间定位等多个基准上取得了最先进性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床实用的胸部X光系统需要可调阈值的分类、空间定位和解剖测量能力，而现有VLMs要么不处理这些问题要么将它们视为独立问题，存在生成模型与实际放射学需求之间的差距。

Method: CARE-X通过三个关键设计实现：(1) 在生成骨干网络上增加焦点损失分类头和组合损失定位头，与语言建模目标联合训练；(2) 提出DAPO（解耦裁剪与动态采样策略优化），利用任务特定的奖励信号优化报告生成、VQA和空间定位；(3) 将Qwen3-VL-4B-Instruct与本地工具调用能力耦合，用于调用确定性测量工具进行解剖测量。

Result: 在四个报告生成基准的大多数指标上取得最先进性能；ReXVQA上达到94.0%准确率（比次优基线高6.0个百分点）；生成式空间解码与专用检测头精度接近；在五个测量依赖条件下，混合推理相比纯感知基线平均F1提升43.6个百分点。

Conclusion: 结构化预测与生成任务能够相互促进，统一的辅助监督和奖励对齐生成可以显著提升医学影像基础模型的临床实用性，而工具调用结合VLMs是解决测量依赖诊断的有效路径。

Abstract: A clinically useful chest X-ray system must go beyond fluent report generation: it should classify findings with tunable decision thresholds, localize them spatially, and derive the anatomical measurements upon which many diagnoses depend. Today's Vision-Language Models (VLMs) treat these as separate problems, if they address them at all, leaving a gap between what radiologists need and what generative models provide. We introduce CARE-X, a chest X-ray VLM that narrows this gap by unifying auxiliary discriminative supervision with reward-aligned generation. CARE-X augments its generative backbone with focal-loss classification and composite-loss grounding heads, co-trained alongside the language-modeling objective. This auxiliary supervision produces discriminative diagnostic predictions with tunable decision thresholds and precise spatial localization while also improving report quality, providing evidence that structured prediction and generation reinforce one another. Building on this foundation, Decoupled Clip and Dynamic Sampling Policy Optimization (DAPO) leverages task-specific reward signals for report generation, visual question answering (VQA), and spatial grounding, directly optimizing the clinical quality metrics that matter in practice. The result is state-of-the-art performance on the majority of metrics across four report-generation benchmarks, 94.0% VQA accuracy on ReXVQA (+6.0 pp over the next-best baseline), and generative spatial decoding that reaches near parity with dedicated detection heads. Separately, to address measurement-dependent diagnoses, we couple Qwen3-VL-4B-Instruct with native tool-calling capabilities for invoking deterministic measurement tools, while retaining full visual access to the image. This hybrid inference yields +43.6 pp average F1 over perception-only baselines across five measurement-dependent conditions.

</details>


### [112] [NCGR: Noise-Conditional Gated Rectification for Camera Extrinsic Perturbations in BEV 3D Object Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03895)
*Wenbin Pan,Wanhao Liu,Liwei Luo,Panshuo Li,Yong Xu,Renquan Lu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: NCGR是一种针对相机外参扰动下BEV 3D检测的噪声条件门控修正方法，通过预测2D修正偏移和相机级门控来补偿投影误差，无需显式估计完整的6自由度外参修正，在动态外参扰动下显著优于基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于相机的BEV 3D检测通常假设相机外参准确且固定，但实际中外参扰动会导致空间交叉注意力（SCA）中BEV参考点的图像平面投影偏移，使查询从错误区域采样特征，严重降低检测性能。

Method: 为每个查询-相机对预测2D修正偏移，并通过相机级门控调制以在原生可变形采样前修正基础投影。训练阶段通过计划插值将基于扰动的条件/门控构建量逐步替换为由相机特征预测的辅助标量对应物，实现无扰动元数据的盲推理。同时使用权重共享的干净教师/扰动学生分支对，通过BEV一致性目标监督修正模块。

Result: 在nuScenes的五相机动态外参压力测试中，NCGR达到39.69% NDS，显著高于BEVFormer的28.00%和CAPE的33.23%；在干净外参下性能与BEVFormer相当。

Conclusion: NCGR有效缓解了相机外参扰动对BEV 3D检测的负面影响，在无需显式估计完整外参修正的前提下实现鲁棒检测，且不牺牲干净外参条件下的性能。

Abstract: Camera-based bird's-eye-view (BEV) 3D detection typically assumes accurate and fixed camera extrinsics. In detectors using spatial cross-attention (SCA), extrinsic perturbations displace the image-plane projections of BEV reference points, causing queries to sample features from incorrect regions and degrading detection performance. To address this failure mode, Noise-Conditional Gated Rectification (NCGR) is proposed to compensate for projection errors without explicitly estimating a full six-degree-of-freedom extrinsic correction. For each query-camera pair, a 2D rectification offset is predicted and modulated by a camera-level gate to rectify the base projection before native deformable sampling. During training, the perturbation-derived quantities used to construct the condition and gate are gradually replaced through scheduled interpolation by counterparts generated from an auxiliary scalar predicted from camera features. This transition enables blind inference without perturbation metadata. During training, a weight-shared clean-teacher/perturbed-student pair is used, and the rectification module is supervised by a BEV-consistency objective between the two branches. NCGR is evaluated on nuScenes with simulated dynamic and static extrinsic perturbations. In a five-camera dynamic stress test, NCGR achieves 39.69% NDS, compared with 28.00% for BEVFormer and 33.23% for CAPE. Under clean extrinsics, NCGR maintains performance comparable to that of BEVFormer.

</details>


### [113] [UniEvo-RS: Omni-Prompt Unified Remote Sensing Segmentation with Representative Exemplar-Driven Prototype Evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03911)
*Kunquan Zhang,Peilang Li,Xikun Hu,Yunkai Yang,Yushan Zou,Zhiwei Zhang,Runmin Dong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出UniEvo-RS，一个统一提示的遥感图像分割框架，通过代表性样本驱动的原型演化机制，支持文本和视觉提示的统一架构及动态任务路由，在少量人工标注交互下实现无需训练的类别自适应性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的提示驱动视觉语言模型在新型场景、未见类别和视觉混淆背景上性能严重退化；且现有统一范式主要依赖图像内特定提示，缺乏灵活的任务路由以适应多意图工作流。

Method: 构建多指令提示数据集，在单一架构中统一文本驱动和视觉驱动提示，并建立动态任务路由机制；引入代表性反馈驱动的无训练原型演化机制，通过对比样本上的手动标注与初始预测，将预测误差提炼为正负原型，增强LLM查询召回并抑制空间背景噪声。

Result: 实验表明UniEvo-RS统一了多种提示任务，在大多数设置下达到最先进性能；在少量代表性样本交互下，能在批处理标注过程中对未见类别实现无需训练的渐进式精度提升。

Conclusion: UniEvo-RS通过原型演化机制有效解决了遥感分割中静态模型的泛化瓶颈，以极小的交互成本实现了面向大规模批处理标注的持续性能改进。

Abstract: Prompt-driven vision-language models (VLMs) hold immense promise for accelerating dense remote sensing (RS) annotation, but static models suffer from severe performance degradation when deployed on novel scenes, unseen categories, or visually confusing backgrounds. Moreover, existing unified paradigms primarily rely on intra-image specific prompts, lacking flexible task routing to adapt to multi-intent operational workflows. In practical batch mapping, annotators typically refine a small set of representative samples before processing large datasets. Motivated by this practice, we propose UniEvo-RS, an omni-prompt unified RS segmentation framework equipped with representative exemplar-driven prototype evolution. First, we construct a multi-instruction prompt dataset that unifies text-driven and visual-driven prompts within a single architecture, establishing a dynamic task-routing mechanism for highly diverse RS annotation scenarios. Second, we introduce a representative feedback-driven, training-free prototype evolution mechanism. By contrasting manual annotations with initial predictions on exemplars, UniEvo-RS distills prediction errors into positive and negative prototypes. These prototypes enhance LLM query recall and suppress spatial background noise under a fixed-budget clustering memory. Extensive experiments show that UniEvo-RS unifies diverse prompting tasks, achieving state-of-the-art performance across most settings. Crucially, with minimal interaction on a few exemplars, it enables training-free, progressive accuracy enhancement on unseen categories during batch annotation.

</details>


### [114] [StreamDAM: Presence-Aware Memory for Real-Time Streaming Video Object Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03912)
*Xiang Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个面向流式协议的视频目标分割（VOS）追踪器，解决了现有高精度追踪器（如DAM4SAM）在30fps实时流式场景下因记忆管线过慢而性能崩溃的问题。该方法通过模型内优化使记忆机制达到帧率运行，并用单一学习到的存在信号统一控制记忆写入、读取范围、输出抑制和重新检测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VOS追踪器在离线评测中精度领先，但在真实流式场景（30fps、按时钟计时、错过预算则返回上一帧掩码）下性能急剧下降。根本原因在于其记忆管线（memory pipeline）过于昂贵，且输出无法判断目标是否仍然存在。先前方法仅通过"事后补救"（bolted-on fallback）处理，未从根源解决问题。

Method: 提出\method{}方法：(1) 将记忆机制的优化嵌入模型内部，使其运行速度达到帧率，而非依赖外部回退策略；(2) 引入单一学习到的存在信号（presence signal），该信号统一控制四个关键决策：哪些内容进入记忆、追踪器回读多远的记忆、何时抑制输出、何时重新检测目标。通过机制分析发现固定策略无法同时应对"目标消失"和"目标难以看见"两种场景，因此需要逐帧自适应决策。

Result: 在四个基准数据集和五个现代基线上，\method{}成为最强的流式追踪器；在时钟约束下恢复了离线模型绝大部分精度；在最困难的内容上甚至超过了其构建所基于的离线模型。

Conclusion: 记忆管线的流式化重构和逐帧可学习的控制信号是解决VOS追踪器在实时流式场景下性能退化的关键。该工作表明，离线精度排名不能直接推断流式性能，需要为流式场景重新设计追踪器的核心机制。

Abstract: Quality-tier video object segmentation (VOS) trackers such as DAM4SAM top accuracy leaderboards, but they are measured offline, one frame at a time with no clock. Under an honest streaming protocol at 30 frames per second, where a frame that misses its budget is served the last mask already computed, the winner collapses: the rich memory that makes it accurate is too slow to keep up, and what it emits is blind to whether the object is even present. We trace both failures to one place, the tracker's memory pipeline, and rebuild it for streaming. \method{} makes the memory machinery itself run at frame rate through in-model optimization rather than a bolted-on fallback, and governs it with a single learned presence signal that decides what enters memory, how far back the tracker reads, when to withhold output, and when to re-detect. A mechanism analysis shows why a fixed policy cannot win: the control that helps when an object truly disappears is the one that hurts when it is merely hard to see, so the choice must be made per frame. Across four benchmarks and five modern baselines, \method{} is the strongest streaming tracker, recovers nearly all of the offline model's accuracy under the clock, and on the hardest content exceeds the offline model it is built from.

</details>


### [115] [When and Where to Look: Adaptive Visual Evidence Scheduling for Efficient Long Video Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03918)
*Ke Li,Jiayu Chen,Maoliang Li,Zihao Zheng,Hailong Zou,Hengyi Zhang,Xuanzhe Liu,Xiang Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: EcoFrame是一个无需训练的框架，通过熵门控的预算调度和注意力引导的候选提案，实现低开销的查询自适应视觉证据调度，在多个长视频理解基准上取得了更好的精度-效率平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长视频理解需要VLM在少量帧上推理，但现有的相关性方法采用静态一次性选择（固定帧预算和候选池），而基于智能体的调度器通过昂贵的多轮推理实现自适应性，缺乏高效的自适应证据调度方案。

Method: EcoFrame包含两个核心组件：(1) 熵门控的预算调度：利用VLM的输出不确定性来判断当前证据是否充分，充分时提前停止，不足时逐步扩大帧预算；(2) 注意力引导的候选提案：将帧级注意力转化为时间先验，在信息丰富的区域进行密集局部搜索，同时当注意力分散时保持全局覆盖。

Result: 在Video-MME、LongVideoBench和MLVU基准上，EcoFrame在多个VLM骨干上实现了更好的精度-效率权衡。在Qwen2.5-VL上达到64.4的平均准确率（超过BOLT的63.5），并提供比AKS和BOLT快1.85倍的加速；与基于智能体的A.I.R.相比，在保持可比精度的同时实现最高13.5倍的推理加速。

Conclusion: EcoFrame证明了通过利用VLM自身的推理反馈（不确定性熵和注意力）来实现训练免费的查询自适应视觉证据调度是可行的，为长视频理解提供了新的高效范式。

Abstract: Efficient long-video understanding requires vision--language models (VLMs) to reason over a small number of frames selected as sparse visual evidence. Existing relevance-based methods rely on static one-shot selection with fixed frame budgets and candidate pools, while agent-based schedulers achieve adaptivity through costly multi-round reasoning and interactive search. We propose EcoFrame, a training-free framework for low-overhead query-adaptive visual evidence scheduling. EcoFrame leverages the VLM's inference feedback to determine when to increase the frame budget and where to search for additional candidate evidence. Specifically, entropy-gated budget scheduling uses output uncertainty to stop early when the current evidence is sufficient or progressively expand the frame budget otherwise. Meanwhile, attention-guided candidate proposal converts frame-level attention into a temporal prior, enabling dense local search in informative regions while preserving global coverage when attention is diffuse. Experiments on Video-MME, LongVideoBench, and MLVU demonstrate that EcoFrame achieves a better accuracy--efficiency trade-off across multiple VLM backbones. On Qwen2.5-VL, EcoFrame achieves an average accuracy of 64.4, surpassing BOLT at 63.5, while providing a $1.85\times$ speedup over AKS and BOLT. Compared with the agent-based A.I.R., EcoFrame maintains comparable accuracy with up to a $13.5\times$ inference speedup. Code will be available at https://github.com/AK-DREAM/EcoFrame.

</details>


### [116] [Low-Dimensional High-Leverage Subspace Optimization: Beyond Full-Parameter Coupled Training for Neural Network Quantization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03919)
*Peng Xia,Junbiao Pang,Zheng Huang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出归一化仿射预条件（NAP），将优化聚焦于BN仿射参数这一低维高杠杆子空间，分别针对PTQ和QAT设计冻结主干/交替训练策略，以最小代价恢复低比特量化精度损失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低比特量化在紧凑网络上精度严重下降，根源在于全参数耦合训练范式忽视了参数子空间异质性。紧凑网络特征冗余有限，难以吸收量化误差；传统PTQ不改善模型固有量化友好性，QAT全参数联合训练存在梯度耦合问题。

Method: 识别归一化仿射参数为低维高杠杆子空间，提出NAP方法。PTQ场景下冻结骨干权重仅微调仿射参数以提升量化友好性；QAT场景下采用交替QAT-NAP训练范式，解耦特征学习与数值校准。理论分析证明BN仿射参数可完全消除量化失真的通道仿射分量，蒸馏引导的NAP实现方向性平坦度优化。

Result: 在ImageNet和CIFAR-100上的实验表明，NAP能够恢复严重坍塌的低比特量化，持续提升基于重建的PTQ性能，并以极低调优成本超越饱和的全参数QAT。

Conclusion: 揭示了目标化低维子空间优化的原理，为高效深度学习提供了超越全参数耦合训练的新视角。

Abstract: Low-bit quantization suffers severe accuracy degradation on compact networks, rooted in the dominant full-parameter coupled training paradigm that ignores parameter subspace heterogeneity. Their limited feature redundancy leaves little room to absorb quantization errors. Conventional pipelines adopt monolithic optimization: PTQ reconstructs fixed pretrained models without improving inherent quantization friendliness; QAT updates all parameters jointly, suffering from gradient coupling between backbone weights and calibration parameters. In this paper, we identify normalization affine parameters as a low-dimensional high-leverage subspace dominating quantization robustness, and propose Normalization Affine Preconditioning (NAP) for targeted subspace optimization. For PTQ, NAP freezes backbone weights and fine-tunes only affine parameters under the target fake-quantization graph on full-precision models, proactively boosting quantization friendliness before downstream reconstruction. For QAT, we introduce an alternating QAT-NAP schema that decouples feature learning and numerical calibration, breaking the performance ceiling of saturated joint training. Theoretical analysis confirms BN affine parameters fully cancel the channel-wise affine component of quantization distortion, while nonlinear rounding and clipping residuals form the irreducible error boundary; distillation-guided NAP acts as directional flatness optimization, projecting teacher-student logit mismatch onto the restricted subspace. Experiments on ImageNet and CIFAR-100 show NAP recovers severely collapsed low-bit quantization, consistently boosts reconstruction-based PTQ, and outperforms saturated full-parameter QAT with negligible tuning cost. This work reveals the principle of targeted low-dimensional subspace optimization, offering a new perspective beyond full-parameter coupled training for efficient deep learning.

</details>


### [117] [GeoMAR: Unleashing Geometrically Aligned Features for Masked Autoregressive Blind Face Restoration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03923)
*Lu Gan,Hanyu Yan,Chaofeng Chen,Junqi Hu,Dan Zeng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: GeoMAR是一个基于码本的盲人脸修复框架，通过双输入提取管道获取组件级几何描述，结合KV-Q交换策略的几何先验注入器生成几何对齐特征，并将一步映射重构为多步掩码自回归（MAR）过程，实现由粗到细的逐步人脸修复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于码本的盲人脸修复方法在严重退化条件下常常面临模糊的条件特征和脆弱的预测机制两大挑战，导致修复效果不佳。

Method: 提出GeoMAR框架：(1) 设计双输入提取管道，提取基于组件的几何描述（具有明确、空间忠实的锚点），作为文本先验；(2) 通过对齐几何先验注入器（Aligned Geometric Priors Injector）采用KV-Q交换策略，将文本先验与低质量特征集成，生成几何对齐特征；(3) 将一步映射重构为多步掩码自回归（MAR）过程，实现由粗到细的渐进式生成，利用越来越可靠的上下文精炼复杂面部区域。

Result: 在一个合成基准和三个真实世界基准上的实验表明，GeoMAR与现有方法相比达到了具有竞争力的感知质量和连贯的视觉结构。

Conclusion: GeoMAR通过几何对齐的特征建模和多步掩码自回归精炼机制，有效解决了码本盲人脸修复中的模糊条件问题和脆弱预测问题，实现了高质量的鲁棒人脸修复性能。

Abstract: Codebook-based blind face restoration (BFR) often suffers from ambiguous conditioning features and a fragile prediction mechanism under severe degradation. To address these challenges, we propose GeoMAR, a framework designed to unleash geometrically aligned features with masked autoregressive (MAR) refinement for robust face restoration. For feature conditioning, we introduce a dual-input extraction pipeline to extract component-based geometric descriptions with explicit, spatially faithful anchors. These textual priors are integrated with low-quality (LQ) features via an Aligned Geometric Priors Injector, which employs a KV-Q exchange strategy to generate geometrically aligned features. For prediction mechanism, we reformulate the one-step mapping into a multi-step MAR process. This coarse-to-fine generation progressively refines complex facial regions based on increasingly reliable context. Experiments on one synthetic and three real-world benchmarks demonstrate that GeoMAR achieves highly competitive perceptual quality and coherent visual structures compared with existing methods. The code is available at https://github.com/BRL-SYSU/GeoMAR.git.

</details>


### [118] [Progressive Learning of a Diffusion-based Inpainting Model for Separating Overlapped Fingerprints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03937)
*Noor Hussein,Anil K. Jain,Karthik Nandakumar*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于扩散模型的指纹重叠分离方法，将分离问题建模为图像修复任务，通过多阶段渐进学习，从预训练的Stable Diffusion出发逐步引入指纹先验、部分指纹补全和基于多通道条件扩散修复的重叠感知修复，在两个公开数据集上实现了高概率的指纹匹配。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 重叠摩擦脊纹图案是犯罪现场潜在指纹和活体扫描场景中反复出现的问题，现有方法要么依赖需要强领域知识的基于规则的方向场补全，要么训练不考虑领域特定因素的端到端深度神经网络，缺乏一种能够结合领域知识的高效分离方案。

Method: 将指纹分离问题形式化为图像修复（inpainting）任务，采用多阶段渐进式扩散模型学习：首先从预训练的Stable Diffusion模型出发，随后逐步加入指纹先验信息，再增加部分指纹补全能力，最后提出基于多通道条件的扩散修复模型——重叠感知修复（overlap-aware inpainting），用于重建每个分量指纹。

Result: 在两个公开数据集上的实验表明，使用所提出的基于扩散的图像修复方法重建的组件指纹能够以非常高的概率与其配对的真实指纹进行匹配。

Conclusion: 基于扩散的多阶段渐进式修复方法能够有效分离重叠指纹，且重建结果保持了高度的匹配可靠性，验证了将领域先验与生成模型相结合的可行性。

Abstract: Overlapped friction ridge patterns are a recurring problem in latent fingerprints recovered from crime scenes and in live-scan scenarios where residual fingerprints on the sensor may corrupt subsequent acquisitions. Existing approaches for separating overlapped fingerprints either rely on rule-based orientation field completion that requires strong domain knowledge or train end-to-end deep neural networks that do not account for domain-specific considerations. This work introduces a diffusion-based pipeline for separating component fingerprints from an image containing overlapping friction ridge patterns. We formulate the separation problem as an inpainting task and progressively learn a diffusion model for this task in multiple stages. Starting from a pre-trained Stable Diffusion model, we progressively incorporate a fingerprint prior, add the ability to complete partial fingerprints, and finally propose \textbf{overlap-aware inpainting} that reconstructs each component print using a diffusion inpainting model based on multi-channel conditioning. Experiments on two public datasets demonstrate that component fingerprints reconstructed using the proposed diffusion-based inpainting method can match with their mated counterparts with very high probability.

</details>


### [119] [UniWorld-Design: From Pixel Generation to Layer-Native Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03971)
*Zongjian Li,Zhiyuan Yan,Chenxu Bai,Chen Chen,Haoxiang Sun,Shaodong Wang,Feize Wu,Shenghai Yuan,Bin Lin,Zheyuan Liu,Yuwei Niu,Li Yuan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: UniWorld-Design提出以语义RGBA图层作为图像生成、理解和编辑的基本单元，包含文本生成RGBA资产（T2RGBA）和图像分解为图层（I2L）两个模型，在Crello基准上大幅提升图层质量，支持指令式分层编辑。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统图像生成以像素为基本操作单元，但人类设计师通过图层来创建和理解视觉内容。本文旨在将多模态生成模型从"像素空间"转向"图层空间"，使图像生成、理解与编辑更符合设计直觉。

Method: 提出UniWorld-Design框架，包含两个核心模型：T2RGBA直接从文本生成独立RGBA资产；I2L模型以已完成的图像为条件，结合全局指令和逐层提示，联合生成有序且完整的语义RGBA图层。指令接口支持顶层分解、递归分解和定向提取，使得分层成为可寻址的编辑操作。

Result: 在Crello基准上，I2L模型将逐层RGB L1误差降低37%，Alpha Soft IoU相对提升34%（对比Qwen-Image-Layered）；T2RGBA在CLIP Score上取得最优成绩，超过LayerDiffuse和OmniAlpha。

Conclusion: 以图层为原子单元的生成范式能显著提升图像编辑的质量与灵活性，学习完整语义对象而非可见像素分割的图层在实际移动或删除时依然可用，验证了图层原生设计空间的有效性。

Abstract: We introduce UniWorld-Design, a framework that redefines image generation from flat pixel synthesis to structured visual composition, with semantic RGBA layers as the atomic units of generation, understanding, and editing. Our key insight is that pixels define how an image is rendered, whereas layers define how an image is created, understood, and edited. Just as human designers create and manipulate visual content through layers rather than raw pixels, UniWorld-Design equips multimodal generative models with a layer-native design space. UniWorld-Design comprises two models. The Text-to-RGBA (T2RGBA) model generates standalone RGBA assets directly from text. The Image-to-Layer (I2L) model conditions on a finished image, a global instruction and per-layer prompts, and jointly produces ordered, complete semantic RGBA layers. Its instruction interface supports top-level decomposition, recursive decomposition and targeted extraction, making layering an instruction-addressable operation for agentic editing. Because I2L learns complete semantic objects rather than visible-pixel partitions, its layers stay usable when moved or removed. On the Crello benchmark, I2L reduces per-layer RGB L1 error by 37% and achieves a 34% relative improvement in Alpha Soft IoU over Qwen-Image-Layered. Separately, T2RGBA achieves the highest CLIP Score, outperforming LayerDiffuse and OmniAlpha.

</details>


### [120] [JoyAI-Video-Edit: Real-Time Open-Ended Video Editing with Autoregressive Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03974)
*Yicheng Xiao,Wenxun Dai,Xinran Qin,Lin Song,Maoquan Zhang,Hang Xu,Yukang Chen,Yitong Li,Guohui Zhang,Yuan Zhang,Xuying Zhang,Tommy Zhang,Jianlong Yuan,Peihao Li,Shuai Lu,Siming Fu,Chuyang Zhao,Xin Han,Jie Huang,Wenbo Li,Guoqing Ma,Wei Huang,Xiaojuan Qi,Haoyang Huang,Nan Duan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: JoyAI-Video-Edit是一个160亿参数的自回归扩散框架，用于实时开放式的视频编辑，无需访问未来帧或预设视频时长。通过分块自回归适应、源锚定分布匹配蒸馏（SA-DMD）和长视界自回归蒸馏等技术，在保证源保真度和长期时间一致性的同时，实现了约30 FPS的720p实时视频编辑。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实时视频编辑需要低延迟的因果生成和有限的计算资源，同时要保持源保真度和长期时间一致性，这是一个具有挑战性的需求。

Method: 采用16B参数的自回归扩散框架，结合三种核心技术：(1)分块自回归适应（chunk-wise autoregressive adaptation）以减少训练-推理不匹配；(2)源锚定分布匹配蒸馏（SA-DMD）以在两步生成中保持源保真度；(3)长视界自回归蒸馏（Long-Horizon Autoregressive Distillation）以缓解累积的时间漂移。

Result: 在自动和人工评估中，JoyAI-Video-Edit大幅优于现有流式编辑器，在短视频和长视频上与强离线系统相比具有竞争力。完整系统在单个Nvidia B200 GPU上实现约30 FPS的端到端720p视频编辑。

Conclusion: 该工作展示了通过蒸馏和自回归设计结合，可以在不自损质量的前提下实现实时视频编辑，代码已开源。

Abstract: Real-time video editing requires low-latency causal generation with bounded computational resources while preserving source fidelity and long-term temporal consistency. We present JoyAI-Video-Edit, a 16B-parameter autoregressive diffusion framework for real-time, open-ended video editing without access to future frames or a predefined video duration. Our method combines chunk-wise autoregressive adaptation, Source-Anchored Distribution Matching Distillation (SA-DMD), and Long-Horizon Autoregressive Distillation to reduce train--inference mismatch, preserve source fidelity during two-step generation, and mitigate accumulated temporal drift. Extensive automatic and human evaluations show that JoyAI-Video-Edit substantially outperforms existing streaming editors and remains competitive with strong offline systems on both short and long videos. The complete system achieves end-to-end 720p video editing at approximately 30 FPS on a single Nvidia B200 GPU. Code is available at https://github.com/jd-opensource/JoyAI-Video-Edit.

</details>


### [121] [Video-DeepResearch: Towards the Next-Generation Multimodal Deepresearch Agent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03979)
*Zhen Fang,Yu Zeng,Wenxuan Huang,Yiming Zhao,Shiting Huang,Tianfei Ren,Qi Lu,Qingnan Ren,Qisheng Su,Lionel Z. Wang,Qingyu Yin,Shuang Chen,Zehui Chen,Lin Chen,Zhenfei Yin,Yao Hu,Shaohui Lin,Wanli Ouyang,Shaosheng Cao,Feng Zhao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Video-DeepResearch (Video-DR)，将多模态智能体从静态图像扩展到连续视频流，解决视频场景下密集时空定位与开放网络探索结合的问题。通过解耦的感知-探索流水线和分阶段工具解锁机制，配合SFT+GRPO两阶段训练，在自建基准Video-DR-Bench上达到64.0%的平均准确率，超越Claude-4.5-Sonnet和GPT-5等强模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态智能体在处理视频时存在两个关键瓶颈：(1) 模态偏置——智能体倾向于绕过视觉工具而依赖文本搜索；(2) 参数知识泄露——模型依赖内部记忆而非真正的工具增强执行。这两个问题严重制约了视频理解智能体的真实推理能力。

Method: 提出Video-DR框架：(1) 解耦的感知-探索流水线，分阶段工具解锁，强制在网页检索前进行全面的跨帧视觉定位；(2) 两阶段训练策略：监督微调（SFT）后进行组相对策略优化（GRPO），实现自主探索以突破模仿学习上限；(3) 构建Video-DR-Bench基准，包含200个复杂多跳VQA实例（人类与AI协作标注）。

Result: Video-DeepResearch-35B-A3B达到64.0%平均准确率，超越Claude-4.5-Sonnet（59.0%）5.0个百分点，显著优于GPT-5（52.5%）和Gemini 2.5 Pro（57.5%）；30B-A3B变体达到59.3%，与Claude-4.5-Sonnet相当，验证了训练范式在紧凑规模下的有效性。

Conclusion: 解耦感知-探索流水线结合分阶段工具解锁和GRPO训练策略，能有效克服模态偏置和参数知识泄露问题，使多模态智能体在视频连续流场景下实现更强的开放世界探索推理能力，且该范式在小规模模型上同样有效。

Abstract: We introduce Video-DeepResearch (Video-DR), extending multimodal agents from static images to continuous video streams, a setting that demands dense spatiotemporal grounding coupled with open-web exploration. Preliminary evaluations reveal two critical bottlenecks in current models: (1) modality bias, where agents bypass visual tools in favor of textual search, and (2) parametric knowledge leakage, where models rely on internal memory rather than genuine tool-augmented execution. To address these challenges, we propose Video-DR, featuring a decoupled perception-exploration pipeline with stage-wise tool unlocking that compels exhaustive cross-frame visual grounding prior to web retrieval. Our framework adopts a two-stage training recipe: supervised fine-tuning followed by Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO), enabling autonomous exploration that breaks the imitation-learning ceiling. Furthermore, we curate Video-DR-Bench, a human-AI collaborative benchmark comprising 200 complex, multi-hop VQA instances. Empirical results demonstrate that our Video-DeepResearch-35B-A3B establishes a new state-of-the-art of 64.0% average accuracy, surpassing proprietary Claude-4.5-Sonnet (59.0%) by 5.0 points and significantly outperforming GPT-5 (52.5%) and Gemini 2.5 Pro (57.5%). The 30B-A3B variant achieves 59.3%, competitive with Claude-4.5-Sonnet and demonstrating the effectiveness of our training paradigm even at compact scale. Code: https://github.com/Osilly/Vision-DeepResearch.

</details>


### [122] [Perceptual Anchoring: Prototype-Guided Text Calibration for Training-free Open-Vocabulary Semantic Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03991)
*Wanli Ma,Jiangwen Lu,Qinmu Peng,Xinge You*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为原型引导文本校准（PTC）的免训练开放词汇语义分割方法，通过视觉原型校准文本嵌入来弥合通用类别概念与具体实例外观之间的语义鸿沟，无需额外训练或外部模型，可即插即用地提升现有方法的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的免训练开放词汇语义分割方法通常只改进视觉表征，而将编码通用类别概念的文本嵌入视为固定的分类参考，导致通用概念与捕捉目标实例特定外观的视觉表征之间存在语义鸿沟，进而产生不完整的分割掩码和错误预测。

Method: PTC受感知锚定中符号-感知对应的启发，包含两个阶段：在感知阶段，基于初始匹配分数选择可靠的视觉证据来构建类别特定的视觉原型；在锚定阶段，利用这些原型校准对应的文本嵌入，校准强度根据视觉证据的数量自适应调整。该方法无需额外训练或外部模型，可作为即插即用模块。

Result: 在八个基准数据集上的大量实验表明，PTC显著提升了六种代表性方法的性能，生成了更完整和准确的分割结果，验证了其作为改进视觉-文本对齐的简单有效方法的有效性。

Conclusion: PTC通过视觉原型校准文本嵌入，实现了更准确的视觉-文本对齐，同时保留了通用类别语义和开放词汇泛化能力，是一种简单有效的免训练方法。

Abstract: Training-free open-vocabulary semantic segmentation (OVSS) partitions an image into semantically distinct regions based on arbitrary text descriptions, without learning any additional parameters. However, existing methods typically focus on improving visual representations while treating text embeddings that encode only generic category concepts as fixed classification references. The resulting semantic gap between these generic concepts and the visual representations that capture the specific appearances of target instances often causes incomplete masks and erroneous predictions in non-target regions. Inspired by the symbol-percept correspondence underlying perceptual anchoring, we propose Prototype-Guided Text Calibration (PTC) for training-free OVSS. In the Perceiving stage, PTC selects reliable visual evidence based on initial matching scores to construct category-specific visual prototypes. In the Anchoring stage, PTC uses these prototypes to calibrate their corresponding text embeddings, with the calibration strength adaptively adjusted based on the amount of visual evidence. Consequently, the calibrated text embeddings align more accurately with instance-specific visual representations while preserving generic category semantics and open-vocabulary generalization. Moreover, PTC requires neither additional training nor external models and can serve as a plug-and-play module for existing methods. Extensive experiments across eight benchmarks show that PTC significantly enhances the performance of six representative methods and yields more complete and accurate segmentation results. These results validate PTC as a simple and effective approach to improving visual-text alignment.

</details>


### [123] [ParVL: Parallel Scaling and Expandable Compute Allocation for Multimodal LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04010)
*Yang Yang,Qinyu Zhao,Mouxiang Chen,Xiaohui Li,Lixin Gu,Wenhai Wang,Hongjie Zhang,Wenwei Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出ParVL框架，通过复用现有ViT和LLM骨干参数，在多个视觉和语言分支间并行扩展计算，实现多模态大模型的任务特定优化，突破了传统扩展模型参数或推理计算的方式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MLLM扩展策略要么扩展模型参数，要么扩展顺序推理计算，带来大量内存或延迟开销，且无法改变视觉Transformer和LLM之间的固定计算分配，限制了任务特定优化。

Method: 引入并行视觉-语言（ParVL）扩展框架，复用已有ViT和LLM骨干参数在多个视觉/语言分支间并行扩展；每个并行计算流通过分支特定的prefix参数实例化于共享骨干之上，并在约13B token数据上进行全参数监督微调。

Result: 在相同训练配方的单分支基线对比中，ParVL显著提升整体多模态性能；且最佳视觉-语言计算分配因任务而异。

Conclusion: 核心问题——给定固定骨干参数预算下视觉与语言模态的计算分配——可以通过系统研究得到任务特定的最优解，验证了并行扩展范式的有效性。

Abstract: Existing scaling strategies for Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) typically expand either model parameters or sequential inference computation, incurring substantial memory or latency overhead. More importantly, most existing methods fail to alter the rigid, fixed computation allocation between the Vision Transformer and the Large Language Model components, limiting task-specific optimization. To address this, we introduce the Parallel Vision-Language (ParVL) scaling framework for MLLMs, which scales parallel computation by reusing the existing ViT and LLM backbone parameters across multiple vision and language branches. This framework raises a central question: given a fixed backbone parameter budget, how should additional shared-backbone computation be allocated between the vision and language modalities? We instantiate each parallel computational stream with branch-specific prefix parameters over a shared backbone, and train the entire model end-to-end via full-parameter supervised fine-tuning on roughly 13B tokens. We systematically study the computation-allocation trade-off between the ViT encoder and LLM decoder. ParVL improves overall multimodal performance over same-recipe single-branch baselines, and the best evaluated vision--language allocation varies across tasks. Code is available at https://github.com/YangYangGirl/ParVL.

</details>


<div id='cs.RO'></div>

# cs.RO [[Back]](#toc)

### [124] [Light-Loco-Parkour: Versatile Perceptive Whole-Body Locomotion via Multi-Skill Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02653)
*Hongming Chen,Zhuoran Li,Hongxi Wang,Jiangpeng Hu,Ziliang Li,Peize Liu,QingRui Zhao,Xuhao Liu,Liang Pan,Ximin Lyu,Yuntao Ma,Tingxiang Fan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出Light-Loco-Parkour (LLP)，一个端到端感知全人形全身运动系统，通过单一可部署策略，仅依赖机载深度传感器和速度指令，自主决定行走、平衡、攀爬、下台阶或跨越等动作，无需参考输入、技能标签或手工状态机，在仿真和真实室内外硬件实验中均表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有类人全身控制系统存在明显不足：要么追踪表达性的全身参考动作但缺乏地形泛化能力，要么在线反应地形但未能充分利用手臂、躯干和膝盖等身体部位。作者希望弥合这一差距，实现像人类一样在杂乱地形中灵活移动的全身控制。

Method: LLP采用三项关键创新：(1) 全人形感知控制管线，将RL训练的基于速度跟踪的步态策略与从物体交互动作中学习的跑酷技能扩展结合；(2) 从稀疏种子学习地形条件技能，通过将单一动作扩展为跨障碍几何形状的动态可行、地形配对参考，无需大型动作语料库；(3) 从奖励中学习自主技能转换，仅凭深度和指令决定何时及调用哪个全身技能，无需one-hot技能标签、手工状态机或运行时动作生成器。

Result: 仿真和真实世界实验表明，在基准地形和未见的障碍变化上均取得高成功率，且同一策略可零样本迁移到室内外硬件实验，实现在户外环境中仅使用机载感知和单一可部署策略的自主感知全身运动。

Conclusion: LLP展示了在户外环境中实现对类人机器人自主感知全身运动的能力，仅需机载传感和单一部署策略即可完成复杂地形的穿越，填补了现有系统在全身表达性控制和地形泛化之间的空白。

Abstract: Existing humanoid whole-body control systems still fall short of the way humans move through cluttered terrain: they either track expressive whole-body references without terrain generalization, or react to terrain online while leaving the arms, torso, and knees largely unused. We present \texttt{Light-Loco-Parkour} (LLP), an end-to-end perceptive whole-body locomotion system that closes this gap with a single deployable policy. Conditioned only on onboard depth and a velocity command, the policy decides when to walk, balance, climb, step down, or vault, with no reference input, skill label, hand-coded gate, or runtime motion graph. Compared with prior humanoid systems, LLP makes three contributions. First, it introduces a whole-body perceptive-control pipeline that extends an RL-trained, velocity-tracking locomotion policy with parkour skills learned from object-interacting motions, so the same policy tracks velocity in open terrain, executes whole-body traversal at obstacles, and resumes locomotion afterward. Second, it acquires terrain-conditioned skills from sparse seeds by expanding a single motion into dynamically feasible, terrain-paired references across obstacle geometry, rather than relying on a large motion corpus. Third, it learns autonomous skill transitions from reward, letting the policy decide when and which whole-body skill to invoke from depth and command alone, with no one-hot skill label, hand-coded state machine, or runtime motion generator. Simulation and real-world experiments show high success across both benchmarked terrains and unseen obstacle variations, and the same policy transfers zero-shot to indoor and outdoor hardware experiments. These results demonstrate autonomous perceptive whole-body locomotion on a humanoid in outdoor settings, using only onboard sensing and a single deployable policy.

</details>


### [125] [Semantic Haptic Feedback Enhances Dexterous Robotic Teleoperation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02780)
*Bingjian Huang,Sahar Aseeri,Jonas Schmidtler,Joseph Zhang,Sonny Chan,Andrew Doxon,Jom Preechayasomboon,Evan Pezent,Alberto Rigo,Amir Memar,Nicholas Colonnese,Chase Tymms*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出"语义触觉"（semantic haptics）用于机器人遥操作，通过抽象触觉模式传达机器人状态的关键信息，替代传统高保真感官触觉反馈。研究表明在双手机器人操作任务中，语义触觉在任务表现、工作负荷降低、态势感知和用户偏好方面均优于其他反馈方式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有触觉反馈方法试图复现真实交互中的高保真感官触觉，但受限于传感和反馈硬件能力，且可能导致操作者工作负荷过高。因此需要一种更轻量、更高效的触觉反馈方式来辅助遥操作。

Method: 将机器人状态分为"确认"（Confirmations）和"异常"（Exceptions）两类，在机器人仿真中实现模块化触觉渲染管线，通过气动和振动触觉腕带向操作者传递语义触觉反馈，实现触觉模式与机器人状态之间一对多的映射。

Result: 通过三项评估研究，确定了常见取放遥操作任务中最有效的语义触觉设计。在单手任务中语义触觉与其他反馈方式表现相当，但在双手任务中表现出更优的性能，同时降低了任务工作负荷，提高了态势感知能力，并获得了用户更高的偏好。

Conclusion: 语义触觉作为一种抽象化的触觉反馈范式，能够简化硬件需求，在复杂的双手遥操作任务中优于传统感官触觉和视觉反馈，为遥操作触觉反馈设计提供了新的方向。

Abstract: In robot teleoperation, haptic feedback can be used to help human operators accomplish dexterous manipulation tasks. However, existing haptic feedback methods try to replicate high-fidelity sensory haptics that are felt in real world interactions, which are constrained by the sensing and feedback hardware capability and may lead to higher workload.
  To addresses these limitations, this work introduces semantic haptics for teleoperation, which uses abstract haptic patterns to convey critical information about robot states. We categorize robot states into "Confirmations" and "Exceptions", implement a modular haptic rendering pipeline in robot simulation, and deliver semantic haptic feedback to operators through pneumatic and vibrotactile wristbands. This simplifies hardware requirements and enables one-to-many mappings between haptic patterns and robot states.
  Through three evaluation studies, we identify the most effective semantic haptic design for a common pick and place teleoperation task and compare semantic haptics to other teleoperation feedback approaches including sensory haptics and visual feedback. Results suggest that while semantic haptics performs similarly as other feedback in unimanual tasks, it achieves superior performance in bimanual tasks, with reduced task workload, increased situational awareness, and overall preference.

</details>


### [126] [Toward Certified Functional Safety for Industrial Humanoid Robots: The Fail-Passive Gap and a Feasibility Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02809)
*Caiwu Ding,Tao Cui,Lingyun Wang,Chengtao Wen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文指出工业人形机器人功能安全认证的核心障碍在于"失效-被动安全缺口"（fail-passive gap）：腿部机器人的安全状态需要主动控制，违背了传统断电即安全的假设。作者使用认证的外部安全链（光幕、急停、安全PLC等）精确界定该缺口，并通过西门子安全PLC参考实现证明了传统急停停机的接触器断电机制恰是人形机器人无法接受的。论文在Unitree G1上进行了验证，明确不主张端到端的PL e/SIL 3认证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业人形机器人的发展瓶颈不在于运动或操作能力，而在于腿部平台功能安全认证不成熟。核心困难在于：腿部机器人的安全状态是主动控制状态，这违反了ISO 13849-1/EN 60204-1中"失效-被动"的假设——对行走的双足机器人断电会导致失控跌倒，传统的断电本身就是一种危险。

Method: 使用认证的外部安全链（光幕、急停、失效安全输入、失效安全PLC、无线PROFIsafe）作为工具精确定位缺口；由于外部链是封闭且可量化的（PFHD、DC、CCF、PL/SILCL），残留的不可认证部分被定位到机器人侧的反应链。在Unitree G1 EDU拾放单元（3m×1.5m半封闭工作区）上验证，并贡献了人形机器人特有的主动安全状态分析（跌倒即危险、单支撑停止界限、平衡策略残余风险、ISO 13855分离距离）及带来源标记的时序预算。

Result: 通过西门子失效安全S7-1500急停参考实现，证明可认证的Reaction子系统是基于接触器的断电（停止类别0）——这恰恰是平衡型人形机器人所不能具备的环节。在机器人上托管工业软件定义自动化（SDA）控制器与平衡策略共置，将机器人侧PROFINET/PROFIsafe接收移至标准化IEC 61131-3接口；由于G1板载计算非安全认证硬件，该端点不是认证的安全运行时，这强化而非解决了失效-被动缺口，并将其定位到SDA与平衡策略之间的接口。

Conclusion: 论文有意不主张端到端的PL e/SIL 3认证，而是通过外部安全链的量化方法精确定位失效-被动缺口，明确了人形机器人功能安全的核心技术障碍在于安全状态必须是主动控制的，传统机电式断电安全机制在平衡型人形机器人上不可用，未来需要针对人形机器人的主动安全状态开发新的认证框架。

Abstract: Industrial humanoid robots are constrained less by locomotion or manipulation capability than by the immaturity of functional safety certification for legged platforms. The root difficulty is that the safe state of a legged robot is an actively-controlled state, which violates the fail-passive assumption underlying ISO~13849-1 / EN~60204-1: removing power from a walking biped causes an uncontrolled fall, so classical de-energization is itself a hazard. We term this the fail-passive gap and use a certified external safety chain (light curtain, emergency stop, fail-safe input, fail-safe PLC, and wireless PROFIsafe) as an instrument to locate it precisely: because the external chain is closed and quantifiable with established methods (PFHD, DC, CCF, PL/SILCL), the residual uncertifiable element is pinpointed to the robot-side reaction chain. Using a Siemens fail-safe S7-1500 emergency-stop reference, we show its certifiable Reaction subsystem is contactor-based power removal (Stop Category~0)---exactly the element a balancing humanoid cannot have. We deliberately do not claim end-to-end certified PL~e / SIL~3. We validate the approach on a Unitree G1 EDU pick-and-place cell in a 3m x 1.5m semi-enclosed workspace, and contribute a humanoid-specific analysis of the active safe state (fall-as-hazard, single-support stop bounds, balancing-policy residual risk, ISO~13855 separation) and a provenance-labeled timing budget. Hosting an industrial software-defined automation (SDA) controller on the robot, co-located with the balancing policy, moves robot-side PROFINET/PROFIsafe reception onto a standardized IEC~61131-3 interface; because the G1's onboard compute is not safety-rated hardware, this endpoint is not a certified safety runtime, which reinforces rather than resolves the fail-passive gap and localizes it to the SDA-to-balancing-policy interface.

</details>


### [127] [Staying on Spec: Real-Time Monitoring under Uncertainty with a Maritime Case Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02811)
*Elizabeth Dietrich,Hanna Krasowski,Emir Cem Gezer,Roger Skjetne,Asgeir Johan Sørensen,Murat Arcak*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于数据驱动可达集的实时监控框架，用于在不确定性下评估复杂任务与安全规范，并在海上导航场景中验证其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人系统需要在不确定性下满足复杂任务和安全规范，但现有监控方法要么需要大量数据，要么需要显式的不确定性分布，难以在实际中部署。

Method: 提出实时监控框架，利用数据驱动的可达集来评估规范，降低数据需求；为海上导航实例化该框架，构建数据高效的可达集生成流程，并推导适用于实时部署的监控公式。

Result: 仿真和硬件实验表明，在现实扰动下监控具有鲁棒性，与最先进的度量方法相比实现了更好的风险检测性能。

Conclusion: 该框架在降低数据要求的同时实现了实时、鲁棒的规范监控，为不确定性环境下的机器人安全监控提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Robotic systems must operate under uncertainty while satisfying complex task and safety specifications. Monitoring such specifications under uncertainty remains challenging, as existing formulations typically require extensive data or explicit uncertainty distributions. In this paper, we propose a real-time monitoring framework that reduces data requirements by leveraging data-driven reachable sets for specification evaluation. We instantiate the framework for maritime navigation, where complex specifications arise from traffic rules. We develop a data-efficient pipeline for constructing reachable sets and derive a monitoring formulation suitable for real-time deployment. Simulation and hardware experiments demonstrate robust monitoring under realistic disturbances, achieving improved risk detection compared to state-of-the-art metrics.

</details>


### [128] [Biconvex Optimization for Smooth Minimum-Time Trajectories around Convex Obstacles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02834)
*Peter Werner,Tobia Marcucci,Daniela Rus*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种双凸（biconvex）方法用于凸障碍环境下的最小时间运动规划，通过变量替换将最小时间目标与任意阶导数约束联合凸化，利用时变分离平面处理碰撞避免，将问题转化为双凸规划。通过交替优化最大间隔分离平面和轨迹，仅对当前迭代碰撞的障碍物添加平面，使轨迹能绕开障碍物并逃离局部最小值。该方法保证从简单无碰撞多边形曲线收敛，经无人机导航和双臂卸箱实验验证，计算时间与最先进的基于分解的运动规划器相当，但能处理更广泛的问题类别且对不良初始化更鲁棒。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有运动规划方法在处理最小时间规划时，要么难以处理高阶导数约束，要么计算效率不高或对初始化敏感。作者希望开发一种既能保证收敛、支持任意阶导数约束，又具备任意时间（anytime）特性的运动规划方法。

Method: 1) 通过变量替换将最小时间目标与所有导数约束联合凸化；2) 使用时变分离平面处理碰撞避免，将问题转化为双凸规划；3) 交替优化最大间隔分离平面与轨迹；4) 仅对当前轨迹碰撞的障碍物添加分离平面，使轨迹能够跳出局部最小值。

Result: 在无人机导航和双臂卸箱实验中，该方法可靠地生成高质量轨迹，计算时间与最先进的基于分解的运动规划器相当，同时支持更广泛的问题类别，对不良初始化的鲁棒性显著更强。

Conclusion: 该方法提供了一种保证收敛、支持任意阶导数约束、具备任意时间特性的双凸最小时间运动规划框架，在计算效率和鲁棒性上优于现有方法，具有更广的适用性。

Abstract: We present a biconvex approach for minimum-time motion planning around convex obstacles that is guaranteed to converge, is anytime, and supports derivative constraints to arbitrary order. We jointly convexify the minimum-time objective and all derivative constraints through a change of variables, and handle collision avoidance via time-varying separating planes, reducing the problem to a biconvex program. This program is solved by alternating between computing maximum-margin separating planes and optimizing the trajectory. By only adding planes for obstacles that the current iterate collides with, the trajectory can jump around obstacles and escape local minima. The method is guaranteed to converge starting from a simple collision-free polygonal curve. In our experiments on drone navigation and dual-arm bin unloading, we find that the proposed method reliably produces high-quality trajectories with computation times comparable to state-of-the-art decomposition-based motion planners, while handling a larger class of problems and being substantially more robust to bad initialization. Project page:https://wernerpe.github.io/bmtp-website/

</details>


### [129] [Control Barrier Functions via Minkowski Operations for Safe Navigation among Polytopes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02886)
*Yi-Hsuan Chen,Shuo Liu,Wei Xiao,Calin Belta,Michael Otte*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种针对多面体机器人与多面体障碍物的精确符号距离函数（SDF）公式，并将其与非光滑控制屏障函数（CBF）集成，通过闵可夫斯基运算和凸规划对偶问题计算精确SDF，并利用灵敏度分析推导出统一的解析梯度表达式，从而实现非保守的安全导航与避障。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于CBF的安全控制方法通常将多面体近似为球体或椭球体等保守光滑形状，以获得显式的可微距离函数，但这种近似会导致保守的机动行为，无法充分利用系统的精确几何信息。

Method: 利用闵可夫斯基运算，在无碰撞（正号）和碰撞（负号）两种情况下通过伴随凸规划计算精确SDF；利用2D闵可夫斯基运算的几何性质和伴随凸规划的最优性条件，通过灵敏度分析推导精确SDF的统一解析梯度表达式；将精确SDF与非光滑CBF集成。

Result: 通过纯平移案例和三个单轮车模型场景（不安全初始化恢复、单障碍物和多障碍物避障）验证了框架的有效性。与基线方法对比，展示了该框架能够实现非保守机动和安全恢复。精确的旋转梯度还揭示了由几何与非完整运动学耦合产生的一类此前被掩盖的局部极小值。

Conclusion: 该框架通过精确的多面体SDF及其解析梯度，结合非光滑CBF，实现了对机器人动力学、控制和几何的精确考虑，相较于基于保守形状近似的传统方法，能够实现更灵活、非保守的安全导航策略。

Abstract: Safely navigating polytopic environments while respecting the dynamics, control, and exact geometry of the underlying system is a challenge in robotics. Control barrier functions (CBFs) synthesize safe control policies by rendering the safe set forward invariant, but many existing CBF-based methods approximate polytopes using conservative smooth shapes, such as spheres or ellipsoids, to obtain explicit differentiable distance functions. In this article, we propose an exact Signed Distance Function (SDF) formulation for a {\it polytopic} robot and {\it polytopic} obstacles and integrate it with nonsmooth CBFs. Leveraging Minkowski operations, the proposed method computes the exact SDF via companion convex programs in both the collision-free (positive-sign) and in-collision (negative-sign) cases. Furthermore, by exploiting the convenient geometric properties of 2D Minkowski operations and the optimality conditions of the two companion convex programs, we derive a unified analytical expression for the gradient of the exact SDF via sensitivity analysis. The exact rotational gradient further reveals a previously masked class of local minima induced by the coupling between geometry and nonholonomic kinematics. We demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed framework through a pure-translation case and three scenarios with unicycle models involving recovery from an unsafe initialization and single- and multiple-obstacle avoidance. Comparisons with baseline methods highlight how the proposed framework enables non-conservative maneuvers and safety recovery.

</details>


### [130] [Contact-Driven Localization in a Freeform Robotic Self-Assembled Structure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02895)
*Mohammadali Rashidioun,Michael Sosa,Petras Swissler*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于接触驱动的模块化机器人定位方法，仅利用二进制接触信息（机器人间是否物理连接）和虚拟力框架，在无外部基础设施的条件下实现自重构系统的准确相对定位。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自重构机器人系统在形成多样结构时需要准确识别相对位置，但现有方法依赖外部追踪基础设施或高成本传感器，限制了可扩展性和在非结构化环境中的部署。

Method: 利用机器人间的二进制接触信息（是否物理连接）作为唯一通信线索，设计虚拟力框架：机器人迭代优化自身位姿，对已连接的邻居产生吸引力，对未连接的邻居产生排斥力，从而逐步收敛到准确位置。

Result: 在塔状结构和悬臂结构的装配仿真中，该方法实现了有效的定位，支持准确、可扩展、自由形态的自组装。

Conclusion: 该方法无需外部基础设施，仅依赖最少的机载传感即可实现模块化机器人的准确定位，为大规模自重构系统在非结构化环境中的部署提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Accurate localization remains a key challenge in swarm robotics, particularly for self-reconfigurable systems that must identify relative positions to form diverse structures. Most existing approaches rely on external tracking infrastructure or high-cost sensors, which limit scalability and deployment in unstructured environments. In this paper, we propose a novel contact-driven localization method for modular robots that leverages only local communication through binary contact information (whether two robots are physically connected or not). To exploit these contact cues, we introduce a virtual-force framework in which robots iteratively refine their poses attracting toward dock-connected neighbors and repelling from non-connected ones. The method requires no external infrastructure and relies only on minimal onboard sensing. Simulations show effective localization during the assembly of towers and cantilevers, enabling accurate, scalable, free-form self-assembly.

</details>


### [131] [DeRP: An Algorithm for Self-Assembly of Power-Delivery Networks using Recursive Branching in Information-Limited Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02904)
*Mohammadali Rashidioun,Sangwoo Park,Petras Swissler*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为Dendritic Recursive Pivoting (DeRP)的分布式框架，用于机器人群体中多目标网络构建。该框架仅依赖局部通信和基于方位的感知，使机器人作为导管从公共电源自组装电力网络，在局部选择的枢轴点形成分支（近似斯坦纳点），递归执行分支操作以实现可扩展和自适应的网络构建，无需全局规划。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在非结构化野外环境中，通过预先规划的布线网络或电池方案向分布式设备持续供电面临巨大的基础设施和物流挑战，需要一种去中心化的自适应电力输送方案。

Method: 提出DeRP框架：机器人作为导体自组装电力网络，在局部选择的枢轴点形成分支，近似斯坦纳树的斯坦纳点，递归执行分支操作实现多Sink的高效路由。与传统需要全局Sink位置信息的MST和Steiner树方法不同，该方法仅依赖局部通信和方位感知。

Result: 网络总长度渐近接近全局最小长度的约125%，功率损耗相对欧几里得斯坦纳树降低至65%。当Sink数量增加至100时，仿真完成时间呈次线性扩展。

Conclusion: DeRP能够在传统基础设施部署困难的环境中实现弹性、自适应的电力输送，具有可扩展性和无需全局规划的优势。

Abstract: Delivering sustained power to distributed equipment in unstructured field environments using pre-planned wired networks or battery-based solutions presents significant infrastructure and logistics challenges. This paper presents Dendritic Recursive Pivoting (DeRP), a decentralized framework for multi-target network formation in robot swarms based solely on local communication and bearing-based sensing toward sinks. We envision a system in which robots, acting as a conduit, self-assemble a power network from a common source, forming branches at locally selected pivot points that approximate the Steiner points of Steiner trees to efficiently route to multiple Sinks. This branching operation is performed recursively to enable scalable and adaptive network formation without global planning. The proposed method is evaluated in terms of the total network length and estimated power loss, and is quantitatively compared against global baselines such as the Minimum Spanning Tree and Steiner tree solutions (GeoSteiner), which require complete knowledge of Sink locations. Specifically, we found that the networks formed by DeRP asymptotically form approximately 125\% of the global minimum length while reducing power losses to 65\% relative to Euclidean Steiner trees. In addition, we empirically characterize scaling behavior by measuring simulation completion time as the number of Sinks and robots increases, and find that this scaling was sub-linear for up to 100 sinks. The proposed approach enables resilient, adaptive power delivery in environments where deployment of traditional infrastructure is challenging.

</details>


### [132] [ValueFormer: A Causal Transformer Value Function with Stage-Aware Labels for Semi-Autonomous Vision-Language-Action Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02958)
*Inkyu Sa,Konstantin Stulov,Rajat Bhageria*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出ValueFormer，一种策略无关的因果Transformer，在冻结的DINOv3骨干上同时输出平滑的蒙特卡洛价值和尖锐的二元失败检测信号，用于VLA策略的密集逐帧监督。在真实机器人三明治组装任务（1427个回合）中，基于批评者的逐帧训练权重将任务完成率从70%提升至85%，并通过批量化bf16编码器将实时服务成本降低3-5倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 行为克隆训练的VLA策略会静默失败：仅从动作流无法区分正在崩溃的轨迹和正常进展的轨迹，因为模仿学习不提供进展的概念。强化学习不切实际（真实机器人经验昂贵、可变形食物难以仿真），而终端成功/失败信号过于稀疏，无法指出失败发生的时刻。因此作者认为关键瓶颈在于逐帧标签的设计而非网络架构。

Method: 提出ValueFormer，一个紧凑的策略无关因果Transformer，基于冻结的DINOv3骨干网络，单次前向传播输出两个逐帧信号：(1) 平滑的蒙特卡洛价值V_mc用于优势估计；(2) 尖锐的二元价值用于在线错误检测。失败回合使用阶段感知的"成功-然后衰减"回报标注，保留失败阶段前的成功曲线；检测监督来自错误区间而非单一失败时刻，使得策略能恢复的错误也携带监督信号。

Result: 在真实机器人双机械臂三明治组装任务（1427个回合）上，批评者派生的逐帧训练权重将任务完成率从70%提升至85%（n=20时在噪声范围内）；批量化bf16编码器使实时服务成本降低3-5倍，批评者与策略可在单GPU上以2Hz并行运行。

Conclusion: 逐帧密集标签（而非架构）是VLA行为克隆失败检测与改进的关键；ValueFormer证明了在低成本条件下（仅需终端成功/失败位）通过学习密集、连续且正确塑形的价值信号，可以有效提升真实机器人任务的策略性能并支持在线错误检测。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) policies trained by behavior cloning fail silently: from the action stream alone, a collapsing rollout looks much like one making clean progress, because imitation supplies no notion of progress. Reinforcement learning would supply one, but it is impractical here, where real-robot experience is costly and deformable food resists simulation. The cheap alternative, a terminal success / failure bit, is learnable in principle yet far too sparse to say when a rollout went wrong. We argue that the per-frame label, not the architecture, is the hard part: to be useful it must be dense, continuous, and correctly shaped. We present ValueFormer, a compact policy-agnostic causal transformer over a frozen DINOv3 backbone that emits two per-frame signals in one forward pass: a smooth Monte Carlo value, V_mc, for advantage estimation and a sharp binary value for online mistake detection, targets that pull in opposite directions by design. Failed episodes are labeled with a stage-aware, success-then-decay return that preserves the success curve before the failure stage, and detection is supervised from mistake intervals rather than a single failure time, so mistakes the policy recovers from also carry signal. On a real-robot bimanual sandwich-assembly task 1,427 episodes), a critic-derived per-frame training weight lifts task completion from 70% to 85% (within noise at n=20), and a batched bf16 encoder cuts the live serving cost 3~5 times so the critic runs at 2 Hz alongside the policy on a single GPU.

</details>


### [133] [EmbodiedVAE: Disentangled Video VAE for Efficient and Controllable Embodied Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02990)
*Jiayi Luo,Hanxin Zhu,Chen Gao,Jiankun Wang,Cong Wang,Tianyu He,Jianxin Li,Zhibo Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出EmbodiedVAE，一种专门为机器人操作世界模型设计的视频VAE，采用双编码器-单解码器架构和非对称时空压缩模块，将机械臂运动与背景环境解耦，实现紧凑且可控的潜在表示，并引入基于最优传输的一致性模块保证运动潜在表示的时间一致性，在重建质量和动作控制精度上均优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的潜在扩散模型（LDMs）依赖于为自然场景优化的VAE，未能考虑具身操作场景的独特特征，导致潜在表示既不紧凑也不可控，从而阻碍了LDMs的高效训练和精确的机器人控制。

Method: EmbodiedVAE采用双编码器-单解码器架构，包含：(1) 非对称时空压缩模块自动解耦机械臂运动与背景环境，实现紧凑表示同时提供显式具身潜在表示以支持细粒度动作控制；(2) 基于最优传输的一致性模块，显式强化运动保真度和帧间一致性。

Result: 实验表明EmbodiedVAE在高压缩率下实现了优异的重建质量，并在机器人操作场景中实现了更精确的动作控制，相比最先进的视频VAE平均提升了2dB的PSNR。

Conclusion: 通过为具身操作场景定制视频VAE架构，EmbodiedVAE有效解决了通用VAE在机器人世界模型中表示不紧凑、不可控的问题，为LDMs在具身智能领域的应用提供了更优的潜在表示基础。

Abstract: Latent diffusion models (LDMs) have recently significantly advanced embodied learning in constructing powerful embodied manipulation world models. However, despite the remarkable performance, existing LDMs predominantly rely on Variational Autoencoders (VAEs) optimized for natural scenes while failing to account for the unique characteristics of embodied manipulation scenarios, yielding latent representations that are neither compact nor controllable, thereby hindering efficient training of LDMs and precise robotic control. To solve this problem, we present EmbodiedVAE, a novel video VAE that provides compact yet controllable latent representations tailored for the robotic manipulation world models. Specifically, EmbodiedVAE adopts a dual-encoder, single-decoder architecture with an asymmetric spatio-temporal compression module, which automatically disentangles the robot arm's motion from background environment, resulting in overall compactness while providing explicit embodied latent to support fine-grained action control. To further preserve the temporal consistency of learned robotic motion latent, we introduce an optimal-transport-based consistency module that explicitly enforces motion fidelity and inter-frame coherence. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our proposed EmbodiedVAE achieves superior reconstruction quality with high compression rate, while enabling more precise action control in robotic manipulation scenarios with an average of 2dB PSNR improvement over state-of-the-art video VAEs.

</details>


### [134] [A Wearable Stiffness-Rendering Haptic Device with a Honeycomb Jamming Mechanism for Bilateral Teleoperation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03002)
*Thomas M. Kwok,Bohan Zhang,Wai Tuck Chow*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于蜂窝夹紧机构的轻量级（20克）可穿戴触觉设备HJ-Haptic，用于双边遥操作中的运动觉反馈，通过30 kPa真空压力实现1.15-2.64 N/mm的刚度调节，使操作者能够根据可靠的触觉反馈实时调整抓握力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 双边遥操作中缺乏有效的运动觉（刚度）反馈，传统触觉设备通常笨重、庞大，难以实现轻量化和可穿戴，限制了操作者对物体刚度的真实感知。

Method: 设计基于蜂窝夹紧机构的HJ-Haptic可穿戴触觉设备，利用真空压力（30 kPa）改变机构刚度（1.15-2.64 N/mm），并将其集成到遥操作框架中，通过三点弯曲测试和遥操作物体抓取实验评估其性能。

Result: 实验显示遥操作运动、刚度渲染和交互力反馈具有较小的RMSE和强相关性；设备能根据实时夹爪反馈有效调节刚度，模拟直接用手抓握物体的感觉，且真空压力设计确保安全。

Conclusion: 蜂窝夹紧机制在增强触觉反馈方面具有显著潜力，不仅适用于遥操作场景，还可扩展至增强现实等交互环境。

Abstract: This paper addresses the challenge of providing kinesthetic feedback in bilateral teleoperation by designing a wearable, lightweight (20 g), and compact haptic device, the HJ-Haptic, utilizing a honeycomb jamming mechanism for object stiffness rendering. The HJ-Haptic device can vary its stiffness, from 1.15 N/mm to 2.64 N/mm, using a 30 kPa vacuum pressure. We demonstrate its implementation in a teleoperation framework, enabling operators to adjust grip force based on a reliable haptic feedback on object stiffness.
